Started 2026-09-11. The user judges the overall GraphChecker pipeline design sound but the static→dynamic handoff bloated: candidate skeleton → pass1 activation → selected skeleton copy → pass2 activation → runtime-refined graph copy → finalizer mints occurrences → planner re-mints and cross-checks → runtime plan. Goal: keep only what linked replay, VM propagation and symbolic merging need; delete old redundant code outright (no compatibility shims). Hard metric added 2026-09-11: the refactor must reduce total source line count (measure with `find src -name '*.java' | xargs wc -l` before/after); functionality and correctness unchanged.

Acceptance: every campaign under experiments/hadoop/{hdfs-11741,17897,17899,17960} still produces a candidate.smt2 with the same causal chain and the same Z3 verdict as before the refactor. Outputs go to a fresh CAUSYNTH_OUTPUT_DIR (schedule/final/candidate.smt2). Baseline scripts live in the session scratchpad under baseline/ (run_all.sh, compare.sh).

Scope widened 2026-09-12: after the handoff, also audit and refactor the concolic chain (linked replay / VM propagation / symbolic merging / SMT composition) for redundancy and design flaws; the user explicitly allows design changes where something is unreasonable, subject to the same experiment oracle.

Constraint (2026-09-12): on-disk intermediate artifacts that help debugging (refined graph, path closures, manifests, quarantined summaries, topology deltas, etc.) are intentional and stay. Refactor targets are duplicate computations, repeated cross-validation, and write-only fields — not the debugging artifacts themselves.

Design decisions confirmed by user 2026-09-12: (1) two-pass tracing (lite pass1 to select activated regions, rich pass2) is REQUIRED — full rich instrumentation blows up the trace; never propose removing pass1. (2) User agrees with and authorizes redesign of: thin consumer-driven runtime-plan contract, single authority for identities (occurrence/activation/source) instead of derive-twice-and-compare, thinning the shell orchestration layer, replacing stringly-typed identity parsing with one parser. Core mechanisms (VM propagation, consumer anchors/GuardedRead, bounded loop composition, immutable iterations) stay.

Scope widened again 2026-09-12: the custom concolic VM (third_party/OpenJDK-Concolic-Execution-Engine, hotspot concolic/ + prims/jvm.cpp + System.c) is in scope too — delete redundancy, fix bugs, and keep the built image in sync with the source tree (rebuild/re-image only when no campaign is running).

ENGINE DEFECT (found 2026-09-12 09:30, pre-existing on main): EXPLORE closure loop has no wave bound and never converges on 4/6 cases (bug3's convergent baseline was luck). Mechanism: obligation identity (queryKey) embeds familySnapshotDigest/expression-table content that grows every wave (worklist row 5.8 MB→434 MB, DAG 2K→33K nodes) so terminal obligations are re-proposed forever; coverage gaps grow 1→21→42→62; flip queries also order/identity-dependent (UNSAT↔SAT flips). Fixed on fix/explore-convergence (tip 143dc9426): obligation key = sha256(semanticFlipKey + familySnapshotDigest) at RegionPathWorklist:484 was the loop; now decision-prefix identity, per-row reachable definitions (0.68% of the old payload), sorted binding iteration, region_path_max_waves=12 + no-progress telemetry gap; offline replan 54→0; REAL bug3 campaign on the fix: waves 25→3→0, 11m41s, SAT_CANDIDATE/gap 0/z3 sat, chain summary identical to baseline (fatal_path shape 15951b2e…); 11741 running on tip 39f2c1d6f. Note: long campaigns from an agent must be launched under `setsid` (harness reaps nohup process groups → exit 143). Also found: port ids (`.in.<uuid>`) hash the per-run `callId` EVENT_COUNTER (RuntimeSelectedRegionFinalizer ~:2187, ~:2294) → non-deterministic symbol names across runs; replaced by content-stable SiteId+declaredCallee+ordinal spelling (3e33e49e1; two independent recordings now agree 124/124 port ids, recompose shas unchanged). Still per-run: RuntimeRegionPlanner.activationId hashes tid+callId (~:2939) and SharedStateEvidence keys use receiverObjectId/ownerSymRid — follow-up identity item after the current merges. Next after fix: rerun six cases, ff-merge into main, rebuild main jar + VM image, final report.

ORACLE CHANGE (user, 2026-09-12 ~09:30): the previously recorded causal chains/READMEs may themselves be wrong. Acceptance is now judged against an INDEPENDENT understanding of each bug from the HDFS source in /opt/hadoop-* (buggy vs fixed diffs, PRs): is the symbolic root the true trigger, does the chain reflect the real state mutation, is the target predicate the real failure point, do SAT/UNSAT valuations match buggy/fixed semantics. Baseline/reference artifacts are only hints.

USER RULING (2026-09-12 10:35): the @CausynthExceptionTarget annotations sit at the exception throw sites BY DESIGN and are not to be second-guessed; the workloads inject SYMBOLIC RPC faults via manual hooks in each Hadoop bug branch. Acceptance = the buggy version yields a SAT witness whose chain runs fault symbol → stale keys retained → stale key id on wire → allKeys.get(keyId)==null → throw. Work only on engine links that make the target depend on the fault root; do not critique target placement/README design.

**How to apply:** Any handoff change must be validated by rerunning campaigns and comparing verdict + smt2. Work is delegated per [[agent-model-split]].
